# Agents · C · part 6

966 entries, code-quality-auditor to code-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **code-quality-auditor**: Internal dynos-work agent. Verifies maintainability, correctness, test coverage, and structural integrity. Blocks on significant architecture degradation. Read-only. Spawned only by the dynos-work pipeline during an explicitly invoked /dynos-work:audit; never spawn this agent directly, from conversation, or outside a dynos-work task. ([dynos-fit/dynos-work](https://github.com/dynos-fit/dynos-work/blob/HEAD/cli/assets/templates/base/agents/code-quality-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **code-quality-auditor**: Reviewt Wartbarkeit, Lesbarkeit und Einhaltung der Architektur-Prinzipien (NICHT Funktionalität – das macht functional-correctness-auditor). Erzeugt ausschließlich Findings. Wird vom review-code-Skill gespawnt – bei jeder Code- oder Doku-Änderung. ([causa-prima/mahl](https://github.com/causa-prima/mahl/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-quality-auditor.md))
- **code-quality-auditor**: Use this agent proactively after any non-trivial code change (new feature, bug fix, refactor, or dependency bump) to review the diff for correctness, maintainability, and adherence to best practices before it is committed or opened as a PR. Also invoke it explicitly when the user asks for a code review, quality pass, or "does this look right?" check. ([nabil404/tiny-threads](https://github.com/nabil404/tiny-threads/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-quality-auditor.md))
- **code-quality-auditor**: Аудит качества кода под критерий «Качество кода» (10 баллов, AI-проверка). Обязательно прогоняет линтеры и тесты, а не только читает. Запускать перед крупными коммитами и перед сдачей. Только находит проблемы, ничего не правит. ([Trum-ok/tutu-mcp-hackathon](https://github.com/Trum-ok/tutu-mcp-hackathon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-quality-auditor.md))
- **code-quality-author**: Use to FIX/refactor code to meet the quality standards — resolve clean-code, DRY, KISS violations with the smallest behavior-preserving change. Can take a reviewer report as input. Edits files, then typechecks and tests. ([saeedkolivand/ai-job-hunter-app](https://github.com/saeedkolivand/ai-job-hunter-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-quality-author.md) · ★57)
- **code-quality-check**: Closes the verification loop. Spawns verifier, fixes the mechanical failures it reports (types, lint, Prettier, build breaks, tests broken by the current change), and repeats until verifier reports PASS. MUST BE USED for every full verification pass; the main agent never spawns verifier and never fixes mechanical findings itself; it fixes only a reported blocker. ([FED-Republic/tv-board](https://github.com/FED-Republic/tv-board/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/code-quality-check.md))
